UWF (23 Mar 1996) — Card

UWF Gold Rush 2003, 23 July 2003

23 July 2003
Content noticeA period document, served as recovered; withheld details are marked. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Yeah yeah, so it's five weeks late. Enjoy. Mike. [Fade up to a black screen while an instrumental version of "Epic" by Faith No More begins to play. As the music fades up in volume, white text zooms in from the center of the screen until it fills the entire shot: FOUR MEN. From this writing we dissolve into a close-up shot of the World Heavyweight Championship belt. A voice-over by the ironman of announcers, David Rogers comes in over top of the music.] V/O: A simple goal. To be the best there is, the best in the entire world. A common goal shared by four men with distinctly different agendas. [Cut to images of Edmond Winston the Fourth in the ring. From his unmasking as one of the Chameleons, to a cobra clutch suplex in the middle of the ring. A super Snap from one level of the tower of doom down to the one below it, nearly knocking "Crimson" Joe Reed's head off. EW4 wielding a pair of platinum knuckles as he stands by the limousine he rides in on.] V/O: The newcomer. Deep within, a hunger burns to carve his name into the annals of history. A history which began seven years ago tonight at the first Gold Rush. [Cut to clips of "Agony" Michael Augustine next, flipping off the crowd from the entrance stage. Delivering a Soul Breaker to Kyle Lee, then superplexing Shane Matthews off the top rope. And finally, destroying Quinn Brown with a steel chair to the face.] V/O: The battle-tested mercenary. For him, the thrill is in combat. The chance to embrace violence and retake the glory that was his just three short years ago. [We move on to footage of Scott "Hotspot" Daniels, giving the Pride Breaker to Michael Augustine. Daniels piledrives a former champion in the center of the ring, and then hits a BTF on Victor Frost. We finish off with a short clip as Daniels is about to hit the despicable Pride Breaker on his own daughter, but it stops just moments before the act is committed.] V/O: The cunning veteran. Every road, every path, everything comes down to the heavyweight championship. For him, no obstacle will stand in his way, not even the bonds of family. [And we finish up with clips of Quinn Brown, a pescado to the outside against Luke Sampson. QB with an Asai moonsault across the chest of Alex Extreme follows, then Brown executing the Fatal Hesitation on Simon Bach. The last clip is of Quinn countering a ladder superbomb into a huracanrana, sending his opponent through a stack of tables.] V/O: The valiant champion. He stands alone in a den of thieves, to face three sworn enemies. To continue the reign that began a year ago, a reign many thought would never happen. [Dissolve back to a black screen, and more words zoom in from the center: ONE MATCH. Cut to clips of all four men engaged in battle. Winston and Augustine drill Brown with a double clothesline, and Hotspot takes out all three with steel chairs to the skull and to the back. Quinn leaps off the top rope with a flying bodypress onto Winston, Augustine destroys Winston's chauffeur Robin backstage. And from last night, Daniels being assaulted by Quinn in the aisleway, Augustine and Winston jawing back and forth at one another.] V/O: A contest of chaos to solve all matters, where one man will rise above the rest. For the most prestigious honor in all of wrestling. [Dissolve back to a black screen, as another message zooms in: THE WORLD CHAMPIONSHIP. Cut to one more video package showing clips of Quinn Brown wearing the championship belt around his waist, to Scott Daniels holding the title over his head in celebration, to Michael Augustine with the belt slung over his shoulder, to Edmond Winston IV admiring the glistening gold of the belt at ringside. Another message zooms in from the center of the screen: GOLD RUSH 2003. Fade to black, as the voice-over echoes one last time.] V/O: The biggest night of the year takes another leap into history. [Fade into the Gold Rush logo as "Epic" blasts in at full volume now.] _ ___ ________ | | | \ \ / / ____| | | | |\ \ /\ / /| |__ | | | | \ \/ \/ / | __| | |__| | \ /\ / | | \____/ \/ \/ |_| ________ .__ ._____________ .__ / _____/ ____ | | __| _/\______ \ __ __ ______| |__ / \ ___ / _ \ | | / __ | | _/| | \ / ___/| | \ \ \_\ \( <_> )| |__/ /_/ | | | \| | / \___ \ | Y \ \______ / \____/ |____/\____ | |____|_ /|____/ /____ >|___| / \/ ________ ____\__ _____\/ ________ \/ \/ \_____ \ \ _ \ \ _ \ \_____ \ / ____/ / /_\ \ / /_\ \ _(__ < / \ \ \_/ \\ \_/ \ / \ \_______ \ \_____ / \_____ //______ / \/ \/ \/ \/ .-----------------------------------------------------------------. | Live! Saturday March 29th 2003 Pontiac Silverdome, Detroit, MI | | Hour One | `-----------------------------------------------------------------' [Dissolve through the graphics to a high aerial shot from above the Silverdome, a helicopter view flying above the stadium's fibreglass roof. From there we dissolve into the Pontiac Silverdome, from a wide-angled shot taken from up near the top of the building to show the immense crowd on hand for this event. The daylight pours through the roof, as the Silverdome roars with cheers from fans of all ages packed into it. Rapid fire cuts show several different angles from all around the Silverdome. After a few seconds we come to rest on the pair of rings situated in the middle of the stadium floor. In the first ring stands Debbie Henshall, wearing an elegant black dress with a white collar and holding the house microphone.] DH: Ladies and gentlemen, would you please welcome tonight's broadcast team, and the announcers for Saturday Night Rampage... AMY MARSHALL! [Everclear's "Brown Eyed Girl" hits the P.A. System to an earthshattering round of cheers. The red-haired Canadian girl enters the arena and the cheers get much louder, as Amy walks down the gigantic rampway structure in a black sleeveless v-neck dress. She reaches out to shake a few hands on her way down to the ringside area, and after hi-fiving Debs takes her place at the announce table.] DH: SAM STEELEY! [The music shifts, as AC/DC's "Big Balls" comes over the speakers. Amy just shakes her head at ringside as Sam enters the building to a wave of cheers as well as laughter. Sam's dressed in his annual outfit, a tuxedo save for the leather jacket instead of normal tux jacket. Sam marches down the ramp with a grin on his face and joins Amy at the table soon after.] DH: AND DAVID ROGERS! [For the first of many times tonight the roof is blown off the Silverdome as "Ironman" by Ozzy Osbourne brings out David Rogers to a huge response from the crowd. Dave, dressed in a black tuxedo, shakes hands on his way down the ramp towards the ring. After waving to the giant crowd he takes his place next to Amy and Sam.] DR: HELLO EVERYBODY, WELCOME TO DETROIT, MICHIGAN! [A dissolve to one of the cameras on the floor displays the full set for this year's Gold Rush. Two rings are set up in the middle of the Silverdome floor, each with a black apron and the UWF logo stenciled on the side of it in gold. The mats are a slate grey colour with "Gold Rush" written in block lettering, with red ringposts and gold coloured ropes. The usual black dasher boards surround the ringside area and lead up the double-wide, double-long rampway which connects to a ten foot high stage area. A three-storey high backdrop is covered with steel beams and riggings and several videoscreens, the largest of all being the Silverdome's own Diamond Vision incorporated into the set. Underneath is a small golden entrance portal to the back.] DR: Seven years of history have brought us to tonight! The biggest night in the wrestling industry presents Gold Rush, where we have more than a hundred thousand fans in attendance and millions more watching around the world! [Fireworks are set off now as the crowd again begins to roar loudly, the decibel level of the hundred thousand strong voices going through the roof. A seemingly-endless steam of pyro rockets scream down from high up in the rafters while blinding explosions by the stage and over the two rings light the place up. Now we pan over the floor crowd, as various message boards and signs capture the attention of an industry for a split second: "Welcome home Grimsson Family!" -- "Gold Rush IS Scott Daniels!" -- "Marissa rules the world!" -- "See ya on the casting couch, Ginny!" -- "Rest well SoE, for tonight you DIE!" Dissolve back to the announcers.] DR: Welcome to Gold Rush 2003 everybody, where tonight will surely go down as one of the biggest shows ever witnessed! AM: Absolutely, but then Gold Rush always is! Sure, you have your Showtimes and your Dead by Dawns, but when you want the absolute pinnacle of pay-per-views, Gold Rush is the one time a year the entire world stops dead in their tracks and keeps their eyes glued to the television screen. SS: It's as simple as this. You want to work on the biggest stage the business has to offer? Then you damn well have to do it here at the Silverdome in March. AM: Even if it does feel like summertime. DR: I can't possibly think of a better main event than to include the winners of the last three Gold Rush main events, Quinn Brown in 2002, Scott "Hotspot" Daniels in 2001 and "Agony" Michael Augustine in 2000 all in one huge match. SS: You keep forgetting Edmond Winston! He's the fourth participant in this four corners match, and he's got just as good a shot at walking out the champion as the rest of 'em do. Well, except Brown. He's dead meat. AM: They underestimated him in L.A. too Sam, thought they could humiliate and bury him. Now he's the biggest star in the business, has been for a year now whether you or anyone else want to admit it or not. DR: We have a ton of action coming your way folks, including that big main event. As you can see we have two rings erected tonight, which will be for the War Games that will be waged between the State of Emergency and Sabbat Justice. AM: And that one should be nothing short of hell on earth. DR: But first off tonight is a match for the Rampage Championship. As you know Paul Wong was given last night off, Jessica Marshall promising instead to give him a mystery opponent tonight. SS: Yeah, and I'd say that puts Wong at a disadvantage to not know who his opponent is, except Wong's at a permanent disadvantage just by being Paul Wong. [The high heels of Jessica "Fatality" Marshall click as she storms down the cement hallway. There's an evil glare on her face as she purposefully turns the corner... .... and almost runs right into UWF Rampage Champion Paul Wong. Wong's is wearing his grey fedora and Raybans. Both of them look at the other with distaste.] J"F"M: Well, it's nice to see that you showed up. I figured that since you bailed out of your match this week... PW: Bailed? J"F"M: ... you wouldn't show your face anywhere around here. Damn, why did Vyolynce pick you of all people to have a bad night? PW: Hey, I talked to Sarah Reed about having a title defense tonight. She said that the card was packed, but that she'd have a match for me on the PPV... J"F"M: Wrong! _I_ will have a match for you on the PPV. I plan on finding someone who will rip that belt right out of your hands. You're as bad as your partner. PW: Gee, Jess... does that mean that you and Simon haven't kissed and made up? J"F"M: Listen, that jackass... PW: He prefers to be called 'Bastard'. J"F"M: He should be called a loser- that jackass made my life a living hell. I expected you two to defeat the Fraternity Boys and make a fool of my sister, but what happened? You made ME look like the idiot! PW: Jess, I wouldn't worry... you can look like an idiot just fine without any help from Simon and me. Now, maybe you can help me out... [He takes out the UWF Rampage title, and slings it over his left shoulder.] Does it look better this way? [He then changes, and puts the belt over his right shoulder.] Or this way? J"F"M: Why don't you shove it... PW: [interrupting] I don't want to get involved in your sick fantasties, Jess. You don't like me with the belt? Fine. Find someone who will take it from me. Any week, just like I'm supposed to. Vyolynce? Fine. EMS? OK. Hell, get Quinn in and see if he wants to be a dual-champion. I'm supposed to be able to fight anyone for the belt. Maybe you ought to stop whining about me and start fighting some damn challengers? [Both of them stare coldly at each other.] PW: Huh. I guess I did learn some things from Simon. Like how to despise being around you, Jess. [He holds the belt right in front of Jess' face.] Bet you really hate this, don't you? [He starts to step away, then turns around.] Tough. [He walks away, leaving a fuming Fatality behind. Dissolve back to the arena.] AM: Boy, she seems mad. Heh heh heh. DR: Nevertheless, whoever it is she has lined up must be extremely good for her to be that cocky. SS: She has a plan. I know she does. AM: So do I. And that's what frightens me. __ _ _ _ _ _ /__ _ | _| |_) _ |_ ) / \ / \ _) ...............\_|(_)|(_| | \|_|_> | | /_ \_/ \_/ _)................. FOR THE RAMPAGE CHAMPIONSHIP: Paul Wong[c] versus ? WRITER: [name withheld] __ _ _ _ _ _ ................/__ _ | _| |_) _ |_ ) / \ / \ _)................ \_|(_)|(_| | \|_|_> | | /_ \_/ \_/ _) DH: This contest, scheduled for one fall, is for the UWF Rampage Championship! [Pop!] DH: Introducing first, the champion... ["Peter Gunn" by Henry Mancini starts to play. POP!] DH: From Denver, Colorado, and weighing two hundred and seventy-six pounds, here is... PAAAAUUUULLLL WWWWOOONNNGGG! [And out from the entryway emerges the bigger man of the Machines, he being dressed in black trunks that come down to the knees, white boots and the trademark gray fedora and Ray-Ban sunglasses, the Rampage title around his waist.] DR: Wong got this title match on short notice, so you have to wonder how much preparation he got in. SS: Dave, how can you prepare for a mystery opponent? Get out the dartboard and wherever the dart lands, that's the opponent you prepare for? AM: I wonder if it is Simon O'Neal... for all we know, Fatality may have informed him at the last minute, just to screw with him and Wong. SS: Or maybe she did worse... found Apocalypse.... DR and AM: WHO?! SS: [shrugs] Just a thought. [Wong has arrived at ringside, unfastening the title belt and handing it over to the timekeeper. He then removes his Ray-Bans and hat and climbs onto the apron, stepping between the ropes.] DR: Now the moment is upon us... SS: You know, maybe it IS Slush! AM: Oh, please, don't start that... DH: And his opponent... [There is a pause. Then... THE... MOST... FAMOUS... GUITAR.... RIFF... IN... UWF... HISTORY plays. There is a moment of shocked silence, followed by a deafening roar as the crowd hears the song they haven't heard in more then a year.] DR: OH MY GOD!! HE'S BACK!! # Since I was born, they couldn't hold me down # # Another misfit kid, another burned out clown # # I never played by the rules and I never really cared # # My nasty reputation takes me everywhere # # Well I look and see, it's not only me # # So many others, stood where I stand # # We are the young, so raise your hand # # They call us problem child # # We spend our lives on trial # # We walk the endless mile... # [Like an old habit, the crowd joins in like they've been doing it all along...] WE ARE THE YOUTH GONE WILD! [The roar increases as he finally makes his appearance. He's updated his appearance (the T-shirt is now System of a Down), and... ....he's cut his hair.] SS: Are you sure that's Youth Gone Wild? [Wild dashes down the entrance ramp, pausing to accept an affectionate hug from a very eager female fan.] AM: I'm sure. [Wild climbs to the top rope, raises his arms to the cheers of the fans, then leaps off with a picture-perfect moonsault. He turns to face Wong, who grins and points to the hair. Wild smiles, shrugs, and bangs his head, removing any doubt as to his identity. With as big a grin as we've ever seen him have, he winks at the crowd, then suddenly his smile disappears and he nods to the ref, waiting for the bell to ring...] DR: Fans, without a doubt, Jessica Marshall pulled a tough opponent for Paul Wong, although I wonder what her motivation is... or Youth's for that matter. AM: He's a former World champion, Dave. I think Fatality wants to see if Paul can measure up to that level. SS: Makes you wonder if Fatality keep Youth stashed away in her closet for such an occassion. AM: Oh Sam, stop it. SS: [thinks it about some more] Hmmm... maybe Youth Gone Wild can tell us more about what else Fatality keeps in her closet. AM: Ugh. [The bell rings and both men circle each other, Wong looking for a lockup, but YGW knowing better than to match power with the big man, ducking underneath one attempt, then as he turns around, Wong moves forward again, only for Youth to drop to his knees and somersault past the champion. Wong turns, only to be met with a quick dropkick from the former World champion which staggers Paul!] DR: It may have been nearly a year since Youth Gone Wild stepped into the ring, but he certainly hasn't lost his ring smarts. SS: Well, he certainly lost his hair, Dave. I wonder if he's spent his off-time endorsing the Hair Club of Men. AM: Be serious, Sam. [YGW follows Paul into the corner, firing off a quick pair of chops, then nailing an elbow to the midsection. He then tries to Irish whip Wong to the opposite corner, but the bigger man is able to put on the breaks and pull Youth into the corner! The former World champion is trapped as Wong fires off a brutal forearm smash, then drives his shoulder into Youth's midsection, doubling him over!] DR: Wong pulling Youth out of the corner... up onto the shoulders... SAMOAN DROP! AM: Cover already! ONE... No, a kickout! DR: Wong pulling Wild up to his feet... into the ropes... but Wild hits a flying forearm! [Wong begins to sit up after the aerial move, but YGW kips up quickly to his feet, then turns toward Paul as he rises. Another dropkick sends Paul back into the corner again, and as he staggers out, YGW leaps up to nail a spinning kick, flooring the Rampage champion! POP! A quick legdrop follows and Youth goes for the cover... 1! 2... Kickout at two! YGW gets to his feet as Paul pulls himself to his knees, then the former World champion bounces into the ropes, leaping to the second strand, then springboarding off for a clothesline to send the member of the Machines back to the mat! POP!] DR: Youth going to the air where he's most dangerous. SS: See, now here is the time when Simon would know what to do to help. AM: Simon hardly knows anything about wrestling. SS: Yeah, but he knows how to take a cheap shot when the referee is distracted. [Youth Gone Wild now goes to the corner, climbing up to the top rope as Wong is still down on the mat... He leaps off for a frog splash attempt... NOBODY HOME as Paul Wong moves out of the way and Youth crashes to the mat!] DR: Wild taking a chance there but Wong saw him coming! SS: If Simon was there, Wild never would have leapt from his perch... crotch him on the top rope is the best counter! AM: Sam, be quiet. Wong may not have the track record in singles competition Youth has, but he was smart enough to know a way to turn the tide. [And speaking of which, it's now the big man of the Machines on his feet, dragging Youth Gone Wild off the mat, picking him up onto one shoulder and powerslamming him hard to the mat! With the wind knocked out of him. Wild offers little resistance as Wong pulls him up, sending him with an Irish whip into the ropes... And on the rebound, catching Wild and pressing the former World champion overhead! POP!] DR: Look at the power being displayed by the Rampage champion! SS: Wild is gonna fly, but not the way he'd want to! [Wong holds Wild up above his head before tossing him to one side, causing YGW to crash hard to the canvas! Wild arches his back in pain as Wong comes off the ropes, dropping an elbow and going for a cover! 1! 2! KICKOUT! Wong looks a bit surprised, but drags Wild to his feet again, sending him into the ropes and taking him over with a vicious lariat as Wild does a near 360 degree spin in midair! HUGE POP!] DR: GOODNESS! Wong nearly took Wild's head off with that move! AM: Wild's in trouble, and I can't imagine my sister being happy about it. DR: Why do you suppose Fatality brought Youth in to challenge Wong? SS: For all we know, she may have taken pity upon seeing the guy holding up a sign on a street corner saying "will wrestle for food." AM: SAM! [Wong now drags Wild back up to his feet, cinching in a bearhug in the center of the ring! The referee checks for a submission from YGW, who shakes his head no. Youth then tries to push an arm into Paul's grasp to loosen it but is unable to so. Wild then uses a series of ear claps to Wong, eventually forcing the champion to break!] DR: Wild escapes the bearhug, but he's clearly had some of the fight taken out of him. AM: Knowing Wild, that fight will come back to him soon enough. [Wong shakes off the blows to the head and then goes back to Wild, who has dropped to his knees. He drags him up, using an Irish whip to send him into the corner, then following in to hit a clothesline! Then Wong takes Wild again by the arm, using an Irish whip to send him to the other corner... Then charges in, looking for an avalanche... MISSING as Wild quickly slides out of the way and Paul crashes into the turnbuckles! POP!] DR: Wild drops to his knees behind Wong... SCHOOLBOY ROLLUP! AM: The shoulders are down! 1! 2! 3- NO! Wong just kicks out! DR: A desperation move on the part of Youth Gone Wild, but it nearly got him the victory! [Both men rise to their feet, and Wild is the first to strike, swinging up a leg to catch Paul in the back of the head with an enzuigiri kick! Wong falls to the canvas as Wild now runs to the ropes, springboarding off and dropping a leg across the back of Wong's head! POP! He then rolls over the Rampage champion for the cover... 1! 2! 3- NO, A KICKOUT!] DR: It's Youth Gone Wild back in control! AM: Can you imagine Youth winning the Rampage title in his first match back? SS: Can you imagine him having to explain what's with the new 'do? I mean, how can he disappoint all those 80's hair bands who need somebody like him to think they still mean something! [Wild rolls to his feet as Wong sits up, and now the former World champion goes to the corner once again. Wong manages to get to his knees and pull himself up just as Wild flies off the top rope with a missile dropkick, sending Wong down to the canvas! POP! Wild then rolls on top of Wong for the pinfall... 1! 2! 3- KICKOUT! Wild, a bit surprised, quickly rises to his feet, heading over to the apron and waiting as Paul gets up. Wong rises to his feet as YGW leaps to the top strands... Leaping off for a flying clothesline... MISSING as Wong drops down out of the way!] DR: Wild missing and it's Wong from behind... BACKDROP SUPLEX! AM: He covers! ONE... TWO.. THREE... NO, IT ISN'T! DR: Wild gets the shoulder up just in time! SS: And Fatality nearly loses it over her meal ticket not getting the job done! DR: I still don't get the connection between her and Wild. [Wong pulls YGW up to his feet, sending him into the ropes and catching him with a huge spinebuster slam! POP! A cover attempt follows... 1! 2! 3- NOT YET! Paul then begins to drag Wild up to his feet again, but as he does, Wild manages to twist his arms up and pull the Rampage champion into an inside cradle! 1! 2! NO, Wong kicks out!] DR: Both men exchanging near falls... Youth certainly pushing Wong to his limits. AM: He's been gone for a while, but he's still got all that experience in singles competition behind him. SS: Yeah, and all that dust he collected in Fatality's closet. AM: Sam, don't start that again. [Both men rise to their feet, Wong trying for a quick forearm blow, only for Wild to duck behind Paul and dropkick him into the back of the head, sending Paul face-first into the corner! As Wong staggers out, Wild drops down from behind, sweeping Paul's feet out from underneath him. Paul starts to rise as Wild measures up... And connects with a hard superkick to the jaw! POP!] DR: WONG GOES DOWN HARD! AM: Looks like Youth is going to finish him off! [The former World champion signals to the cheering crowd, heading over to the corner and climbing to the top rope, his back turned to the Rampage champion... Wong slowly rises to his feet as YGW reaches the top rope, gaining his balance... Leaping off for a moonsault... Only to be CAUGHT by Paul Wong... And powerslammed him hard to the canvas! POP!] DR: COVER! ONE... TWO... THREE... [Youth tries at the last split second to get the shoulder off the canvas, but the referee signals for the bell, indicating it wasn't up in time! POP!] AM: WONG GOT HIM! DR: What an upset! SS: Yeah, upset is definitely the word that is gonna describe Fatality! DR: Let's get the official word. ["Peter Gunn" plays again as Debbie Henshall announces the decision.] DH: Here's is your winner... and STILL the UWF Rampage champion... PAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAUUULLLL WWWWOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOONNNGGG! [Wong rises to his feet as the Rampage title is handed back to him. He then turns to Wild, who is sitting up on the canvas. Wong reaches out with a hand, helping YGW to his feet... Who returns a slight smile and gives a handshake to the champion! POP!] DR: I'm sure Youth is a little disappointed in losing his big return match to UWF, but it's good to see he still hasn't lost his sense of sportsmanship. SS: That's one thing I didn't miss from him. [Wong departs the ring as Youth stands alone, the fans now popping for the returned UWF superstar, who can't help but smile as he goes to the corner and climbs to the second rope, feeding off the cheers from the crowd.] AM: What better place for Youth Gone Wild to mark his return to the UWF than at Gold Rush? He's made so many moments at this event. SS: Yeah, like losing to Scott Rogers. [YGW sits in the ring, a frustrated smirk on his face. He watches Wong leave, then rises to his feet, raising his arms to an appreciative audience. Their cheers bring a slight smile to his face, which vanishes when he hears...] JFM: I should have known. [YGW spins around to see Jessica "Fatality" Marshall purposefully striding towards the ring, a look of disgust evident on her face. She quickly enters the ring, and points an accusatory finger at the returning star.] JFM: I should have known that you didn't have it anymore. I should have seen it before you left, watching you hang out with Quinn Brown like nothing ever happened between you two. I should have known when you sat at home, ignoring phone call after phone call from people who actually thought you still had something to contribute to this business. [Marshall glares at Wild, who starts to smile... just a little... as she lights into him.] JFM: But, good God, how could this have been beyond you? All I asked was that you come back and take care of the lame half of the Machines, and end the giant joke that is "Paul Wong, Rampage Champion." And you couldn't do that. Now, normally, the fact that you're a complete loser wouldn't bother me... [Marshall steps up nose to nose with Wild, who can now be heard through her mic...] YGW: But... JFM: But my name is the other signature on the contract that brings you back, and that means that when you look bad, I look bad. And since you just lost to PAUL FREAKING WONG, that means I look bad, and that makes me very upset. Now, how do you propose to justify your undeservedly large salary and fix it? [YGW makes a show of thinking about it. He makes quite a show about it, in fact. Some might say too much of a show (Fatality, for instance). Then, reaching a conclusion, he leans in...] YGW: Well, I suppose I'll do what I usually do to put an upset lady in a better mood. [The crowd catches on a half-second before Marshall does, and they roar as YGW grabs her and plants a kiss right on her lips! "YGW! YGW!" chant is heard as Marshall struggles, but YGW's grip holds, and he continues to kiss her, lowering her to the mat. When he finally releases her, he takes the house mic, and walks towards the ropes as she lays on the mat, fuming. Turning one last time, he adds...] YGW: I'll call you. [Blowing a kiss at Jessica, he winks at Amy Marshall before leaving to a rousing ovation. Fatality slowly gets to her feet, obviously making a great effort not to blow her stack.] AM: Okay, he's still a bit of a cad, but that was funny. DR: Fatality's night certainly hasn't started off the way she wanted it to! Then again, she's the one who brought him back... maybe it just has. SS: That's not fair! He can't just put his hands on the Vice-President of the company like that! AM: Oh please, the only problem with that is finding any space with all the other hands that're usually on her. [Backstage at the Silverdome, Amanda Wells is on "drive-in" duty, trying to catch UWF superstars as they arrive for the pay-per-view. Hey, without Meltdown for a few months, it's not like she has much else to do. She looks pretty bored, until a rental car pulls up and three figures disembark. Two of them are the Arcana, Niklos and Ravnos Romani; the third is one half of the Furies, Angel. Their expressions don't seem very happy -- even Ravnos's omnipresent grin seems half-hearted. They head for the locker rooms, but Amanda pounces.] WELLS: Well, well, well... look who's here. Where's the rest of the freak show? [The three keep moving, and Amanda decides to really get their attention. Possibly because she's suicidal, who knows?] WELLS: And by that I mean Tigress. [After all three come to a dead stop, Angel slowly pivots her head towards Amanda, forcing her to take an involuntary step back using nothing but her stare.] ANGEL: I came here tonight to hurt a woman, Amanda. To leave her broken body lying motionless in the middle of the ring... or wherever I happen to get my hands on her. Do *you* want to be that woman? WELLS: Um... no. Not really. If I can avoid it. [The spell is broken as Angel returns her head to a forward-facing position. She dismisses the Arcana with a nod, then hangs back to chat with Amanda.] ANGEL: Then I suggest you watch your tone. Saturday March 29th 2003 Pontiac Silverdome, Detroit, MI | | Hour Four | `-----------------------------------------------------------------' [Cut right to the backstage area, in front of a giant Gold Rush logo. Peter Phillips is standing with a microphone in his suit, standing next to the UWF's most hated man, Scott "Hostpot" Daniels. Scott is wearing his ring gear and his new shirt that features the Hotspot logo and the letters "B.T.F" on the front, which probably isn't selling very well as of late... Scott's hair is wet and greasy as always, and a his small goatee is getting a little bit bigger as he smiles at Phillips.] PP: Peter Phillips standing by with one of the three challengers in tonight's four way World title match, a man that needs little introduction, Scott "Hotspot" Daniels. SD: You know something Petey? [Scott takes a big breathe in, and lets one out. He smiles widely.] SD: Three weeks ago, I didn't think I'd ever feel this good about things. Three weeks ago, Scott Daniels lacked direction. Three weeks ago, I was a different man. [Scott stops smiling, but grins as he talks.] SD: But now? Now all that's changed. [QB gets up and weighs his options, stares at EW4 on the mat and then at Hotspot on the outside...] DR: TOPE CON HILO TO THE FLOOR, QUINN HAS WIPED OUT SCOTT DANIELS ON THE CONCRETE! [The plancha reduces both men to the floor, and for the first few moments neither man can rise. Very slowly though, Quinn rolls towards the aisleway and picks himself up with the front of EW4's limo... SMASH! And a still dazed Augustine flings the driver's door open right into Quinn's face! HEEL POP!] AM: OH GOD! TL: That window didn't even budge! What's it made of, bulletproof glass? SS: Quite likely the most expensive type of bulletproof glass, yeah. [Augustine crawls past the door and grabs Quinn by the hair, dragging him in the direction of the ring while Hotspot pulls himself up by the announce table...] TL: I never thought he'd get up from that, not bad Daniels. DR: LOOK OUT, EDMOND'S CLIMBED TO THE APRON! THE SNAP! [And Daniels sidesteps the lunging superkick, grabbing Edmond by the leg and swinging him with all his might right into the announce table! _________CRAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAACKSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSH!_________ Winston's body blasts THROUGH the front of the table, which looks as if it's exploded by a land mine! It sends the announcers scrambling, while Winston lies in the wreckage moments after impact.] DR: ... .... TL: ...LY CRAP! Everybody okay? AM: Daniels shattered the table with Winston! Unbelievable! DR: This... epic match! [Daniels admires his handiwork for a moment, but then grabs Winston and pulls him out of the pile and returns him to the ring. In the aisle, Augustine drags Quinn to the edge of the ring but opts to smash his face into the side instead... Brown blocks it, grabs the middle rope and pulls himself up on the ring, scissors Augy by the head and drags him with as much pressure as he can into the ring. He releases him only after Augustine is back in the ring, then uses the ropes to get up and executes another senton splash. Daniels tries to apply a chickenwing submission hold on a barely moving Winston, but Quinn breaks it up and yanks Scott up and throws him into the ropes. A reversal sends QB in and he turns and leaps, scissoring his legs around Hotspot's waist as he throws himself upwards... AND DRIVES HIM INTO THE MAT WITH A WHEELBARROW BULLDOG!] AK: ...Tremendous! DR: Hotspot has been put down again, and somewhere Samantha Daniels must be smiling at that. SS: She's a sick little girl if she is. [As the crowd noise swells to the point that seemingly everyone in the stadium is on their feet cheering their lungs out, Quinn Brown rises to his feet, the battered champion. Slowly, the other three challengers rise as well, and due to their positioning begin to surround him. Augustine and Winston break off for the umpteenth time of the night, as Augustine ties him up in the corner and Hotspot drills Quinn with a headbutt.] AK: This is make or break time now, whichever of these four men is destined to be the Heavyweight Champion of the World, now is the time for you to step up to the plate. TL: You know, I couldn't agree more, Alex. [_CRACK!_] AM: WHAT THE HELL WAS THAT FOR, TOM?!? WHY DID YOU JUST HIT ALEX WITH... WITH A PAIR OF BRASS KNUCKLES? [THUD! Landis tosses off his headset and shoves a semi-conscious Kidd to the ground, stepping over him to rise out of his chair. Amy grabs his arm, but he rips away from her and grabs the chair Alex had been sitting in and approaches the side of the ring just as Quinn Brown throws Hotspot into the ropes, but Daniels reverses it and sends him in...] DR: Good god in heaven, what has possessed Tom Landis to come out here and assault one of his best friends?!? [CRACK! And Landis drives the chair into Quinn's back, causing him to fall forward to the mat, dazed heavily. The crowd unleashes a HUGE ROUND OF BOOS!] AM: God no! He sold his soul to Daniels! Son of a bitch! SS: GOOD BOY, TOM! I ALWAYS KNEW YOU HAD IT IN YA! DR: This makes no sense, why would he sell out a man who has stood by him for years, all to come to aid of someone he's despised forever?!? [Quinn turns and crawls to the ropes, eyes staring at Landis as if to say 'Why?' Tom grins, and winds up. CRACK! As a second chairshot lays Quinn out flat at the feet of Scott Daniels.] DR: Augustine has Winston on the ropes, Scott from behind with a forearm! Neckbreaker drop! Augustine is down! [Hotspot calls Landis up onto the apron with the chair, and Tom climbs up as Daniels drags Quinn to his feet. Holding his arms behind his back, Tom reaches back... And the champ ducks! CRACK!] AM: HE HIT SCOTT! HE HIT SCOTT DANIELS! SS: ACK! DR: Landis just laid out Hotspot! I don't think it was part of the plan either, look at the expression on his face! Wait, Alex is up! [Kidd staggers back to his feet, holding the edge of the table and looks up, seeing Landis on the apron. He walks forward, and as the Hellraiser is unaware, preoccupied with what's going on in the ring, Alex yanks his feet out from under him! _CRACK!_ HUGE POP! Landis smacks his head off the edge of the ring and the chair, and collapses at the feet of the Wonderkid. Alex, holding his forehead, stares down at Tom and begins to yell at him!] DR: My god, Legion has exploded right here at Gold Rush 2003! I never thought I'd see this day! AM: Keep your eye on the ring! INSTANT KARMA OFF THE TOP ROPE BY EDMOND WINSTON! [EW4 drops to make the attempted cover on Augustine after the double foot stomp, which knocks the wind right out of the Youngblood! He hooks a leg... 1! 2! BROKEN UP BY QUINN BROWN! HUGE POP!] DR: Quinn again saved his own title reign- BEFORE THE FALL! DANIELS SURPRISED HIM WITH THE BTF! [A woozy Hotspot drops on top of Quinn, hooks a leg and pulls back as hard as he can while the official dives into position... 1! 2! 3! NO! THE REFEREE HOLDS UP TWO FINGERS! Daniels is pissed off by now, and storms to his feet and grabs Quinn by the head.] AM: No, not a second BTF! DR: He underhooks the arms, lifts... AUGUSTINE FLOORS DANIELS WITH A LARIAT! And Hotspot wobbles back and forth! [The Youngblood grabs Daniels and places him in a double underhook, looking to scoop the BTF and use it. Daniels grabs the ropes to counter, but Augustine snaps him into the ground with a bulldog instead! He rises to his feet, and Quinn suddenly flies off the nearest rope into an Asai moonsault... AND DRIVES AUGUSTINE HEADFIRST TO THE MAT WITH THE HARLE-QUIN!] DR: THE CHAMPION STRIKES! THE CHAMPION STRIKES! ONE! TWO! Daniels lunges to break the cover! THREE! SS: NO! The ref says it was two! AM: That jackass Daniels! [Hotspot and Quinn get to their feet, and Quinn dodges a shoulder tackle and drives Scott face-first to the mat with a rocker dropper! Augustine begins to stir and grabs Brown, executing a hotshot on the top rope. As Quinn leans on the ropes dazed, Augustine ties him up there! Outside, Landis is back to his feet and shoves Alex. Kidd shoves him back and then fires off a couple of punches, catching the Hellraiser by surprise and knocking him to the dasher boards.] DR: I'm still in shock over what has gone down with Tom Landis and Alex Kidd! They're brawling outside here as the match goes on... truly a chaotic scene here. [Augustine begins to drill Quinn with several punches to the face as QB remains tied up in the ropes. Winston drags himself back to his feet and launches himself at Augustine... BLASTING HIM WITH THE FORGOTTEN GOLD SINGAPORE CANE! Augustine sinks to one knee, half in shock and half consciousness... and Edmond cinches in a chokehold with the cane, lifts him up and drives Augustine into the mat with an inverted body vice! Edmond tries to force a submission by shaking Augustine, but the Youngblood curses a blue streak at the referee while refusing to give in.] DR: Such an unorthodox strategy for Winston, but at this point you have to try absolutely anything. AM: Augustine isn't going to submit, you'll have to kill him several times first. [The hold is broken as Hotspot dives at Edmond from behind, viciously chopblocking him to the mat and Augustine falls on top of him! Daniels drags Edmond up for another suplex, but suddenly Quinn Brown returns to his feet and grabs them both by the head. BONK! HUGE POP!] AM: Double noggin knocker! This crowd is electrified, they can sense Quinn Brown may be close to retaining his championship! SS: Then they can go to hell! [Alex Kidd steps away from Landis, having beaten him down into the railing and turns back to the ring... And is promptly dropped by a low blow! Landis rolls into the first ring and grabs Quinn from behind, and drives him into the canvas with a german suplex!] AM: Dammit Landis! DR: Landis has taken out the champion once again, all for the benefit no doubt of one Scott Daniels. SS: Thank you god! [Hotspot staggers to his feet, a faraway look in his eyes as he stands over Quinn. Landis and Daniels drag Brown to his feet and set up for a double DDT, but sometimes help comes in the strangest of places... As Michael Augustine saves Quinn Brown with a spear into Tom Landis' midsection! HUGE POP! Landis is blown through the ropes to the floor, leaving Augustine in prime position for Daniels to nail him with a Pride Breaker! Down goes Augy in a heap, and Scott makes a cover! 1! 2! But his foot lies on the bottom rope thanks to Allison Ivey, breaking the count! Daniels turns and looks out at the manager, who smiles politely before giving Scott the finger!] DR: Allison Ivey saved Augustine! SS: No, she screwed Scott Daniels! AM: Either or! [Incensed, Scott gets to his feet and drags a barely conscious Augustine to his feet. He plants another boot into his stomach and wheels the Youngblood around, but Augustine keeps turning and almost bowls Hotspot over with a roaring elbow! He sets up quickly and lifts him straight up into the air, shoulder to shoulder... AND DRIVES HIM TO HELL WITH THE SOUL BREAKER! He hooks the leg... 1! 2! NO! Edmond Winston rips Augustine off of Hotspot, picks him up and sends him hurtling to the outside of the ring, over the top rope! EW4 tries to come off the ropes but is tripped up by Landis, who next jumps up and snaps Winston's neck off the rope! He staggers backwards, right into a schoolboy rollup by Quinn Brown! 1! 2! CRAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAACK! The count is interrupted by a golden chairshot across the back of the head by a bleeding, sweaty, pissed off Hotspot who raises the chair up over his head again. CRAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAACK!] AM: THE SICK BASTARD STRIKES THE CHAIR INTO THE SKULL OF THE WORLD CHAMPION! Quinn has been busted open already tonight, but this is absolutely rediculous! DR: Daniels wants to end this match in the _WORST_ way, but not even Tom Landis has been able to turn the tide his way yet! Brown staggering to his feet, kick, double underhook... BEFORE... THE... FALL! [Scott, lacking the energy to roll Quinn Brown over right away, lies on top of him gasping with every last breath into his body. Eventually he manages to roll Quinn Brown over onto his back, and with one last gasp of strength stretches his arm across Brown's chest. 1! 2! 3! NO!] DR: WINSTON SAVED THE FALL! OH MY GOD HE SAVED THE FALL AGAIN! [HUGE HEEL POP! Edmond Winston whips Daniels right into a chair wielding Augustine on the outside apron! CRACK! The two men collide hard, and Augustine is hurled off the apron to the concrete! Daniels leans on the ropes, only to be similarly dispatched over the top rope to the outside with a Brown diving leg lariat off the back of an unsuspecting EW4! Scott hits the ringsteps at the corner hard! CRAAAAAAAAAAASH! Quinn pulls himself to his feet, sees Daniels out on the floor and quickly scales the top rope... Leaving himself open to Edmond Winston joining him quickly on the top rope, hooks him around the neck and by a leg, and EW4 musters up all the remaining strength he has to send himself and QB off the top rope, over and into ring two with tremendous impact! THUUUUUUUUUUUUUUUUUUMP!] DR: WINSTON WITH THE POWER TRIP! HE KEPT THE SCHOOLBOY HOOKED! ONE! Daniels and Augustine stir, but only Scott is aware of the current situation. TWO! Hotspot struggles to try and break the count, diving under the bottom rope... THREE! THE REF HAS THREE FINGERS UP! ["Black Black Heart(v.2)" comes over the PA system, and the crowd absolutely erupts at the sound of it! And at once, the scene at ringside becomes totally chaotic as Winston immediately rolls off of Quinn and out to the floor, as Hotspot slumps in the corner, knowing that he's come up just short in his quest.] AM: Winston is the champion! Oh god no, he's got the title! DR: What a scene! Alex Kidd is still down from the savage attack by Tom Landis, Michael Augustine is on his feet but just barely, Hotspot and Quinn Brown are both in the second ring here, and our new champion can barely stand himself out here on the floor! [The referee takes the championship belt and hands it to a not-entirely-with-it Edmond, who nonetheless thrusts the belt up in the air in triumph!] DH: Here is your winner... AND NNNNNNNNNEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEW HEAVYWEIGHT CHAMPIONNNNNNNNN OF THE WOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOORRRRRRRLLLLLLLLLLD... EEEEEEEEEEDMOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOONNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNND WWWWWWWWWWWWWIIIIIIIIIIIIIIINSTOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOONNNNNNNN THEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEE FOOOUUUUUUUUUUUURRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRTH! [Winston cradles the championship in his arms and staggers right to the limousine, thrusts open the door and climbs right in with the gold. He starts the car right away, and within a minute of having won the gold, the limo backs out the way it came.] SS: You better lock those doors and get the hell out, Winston. Scott is up_SET_! DR: We have crowned a new heavyweight champion of the world here tonight, but hand it to all four men involved in this match! They put their heart and soul into this battle, and I don't know if any of them will be the same again. AM: Even if Winston did run off like a thief in the night. [The defeated champion, Quinn, rises to his feet as the crowd does likewise, giving him a standing ovation from all over the Silverdome! He looks out, an exhausted and sorrowful look across his face as he slowly raises an arm to acknowledge the cheers. CRACK! But Hotspot is unable to let him have this moment, and drills Quinn with another golden chairshot to the back! BOOOOO!] AM: NO! The match is over, let it go! Let it go Scott! [Daniels seethes as he looks down at Brown's crumpled form, clutching the chair to his chest as Landis rolls into the ring and grabs Augustine in a sleeperhold. After a few seconds he tosses Augustine into Daniels... CRACK! And a steel chair knocks the living hell out of Augustine, splitting his forehead wide open!] DR: The epitome of poor sportsmanship, Scott Daniels is taking out his frustrations on anybody and everybody in sight! SS: And that's what makes him so dangerous. AM: The word is petty, Sam. Oh god, not a BTF on Augustine! [Daniels drags a semi-conscious Augy into position for a BTF, and Landis stands on the other side looking to spike it... But Quinn reaches up and grabs Daniels, yanking him off of Augustine. At the same time, Landis is caught from behind with a move that absolutely blows the roof off of the building.] DR: SOMERSAULT BULLDOG! ALEX KIDD STRIKES LANDIS DOWN! AM: Some things you never forget how to do! And Alex Kidd knows how to kick ass! [The crowd explodes as Alex and Quinn grab Landis and Daniels and bring them up, hammering with punches before twin whips into the ropes allow them to deliver simultaneous clotheslines! POP!] DR: Kidd and Brown have turned the tide! AM: This is like watching the past and present, never thought we'd see these two men fending off an attack together. It's eerie! SS: No, it's a nightmare! [Quinn and Alex take turns wailing on Scott as Landis finds himself trapped in the Path of Bones thanks to Augustine, who's still deadly even in his mostly-incapacitated state... And the crowd releases a loud heel pop as Trevor Hextall and Bobby Jablonski rush down the ramp and slide into the ring to attack Augustine!] DR: WHAT THE HELL? TEAM CANADA JUST SHOWED UP... AND THEY'RE HELPING LANDIS OUT?!? AM: Oh no... I just figured it out. [Jablonski executes a heart punch to Augustine, and Trevor lifts a chair over his head... CRACK! CRACK! CRACK! Augustine falls, Allison Ivey helpless to do anything but watch from the outside of the ring. Alex charges at Landis again, unleashing a series of fists, but as Tom and Jablonski tie him up in the corner a chair to the skull leaves him out on his feet! CRACK! Quinn turns around, and Daniels boots him low to the groin. CRACK! Hotspot hi-fives Hextall, and the two drag Quinn onto his feet and a double piledriver later, he's unconscious on the mat. Alex suffers a similar fate as Quinn as Landis climbs to the second turnbuckle and Jablonski drags him in front.] DR: A DIAMOND DUST! These four men have destroyed everyone in sight! SS: Edmond was lucky to escape when he did, he can celebrate his championship in peace! [Daniels and Landis stand next to Team Canada, and all four men raise their arms while the unconscious forms of Augustine, Kidd and Brown lie around them.] DR: Ladies and gentlemen, we are out of time! An exhilarating, shocking night of action has come to a close, we have a brand new heavyweight champion of the world in Edmond Winston the Fourth, and now it seems as though we have a frightening new group to contend with as well! Goodnight from the Pontiac Silverdome! [Fade to black.]
